- BANK-MANUAL-05: TigerBeetle immutable transfer and idempotent identity reference. Test original amount/time, late linkage, replay, duplicate rejection and mismatch. Duplicate image fingerprint/region intake, real operator authorization, reversal, cross-device production uniqueness and actual Bank ingress remain separate.

## 105. BANK-DUP — v13.83 (phase 3i)
- BANK-DUP-01: Distinguish source SHA-256, normalized region and region visual hash from transaction identity. Exact source with overlapping region is a conflict; disjoint regions are independent candidates. Image identity already recorded cannot be overwritten by OCR; omitted fields persist.
- BANK-DUP-02: Same account and explicit reference is a duplicate-review candidate. Near dHash (distance <=4) plus same account, exact amount and parsed date is review only; distinct explicit references suppress the visual hint, never an exact region conflict. Same amount alone is not evidence of duplication. Threshold experimental.
- BANK-DUP-03: All unposted conflicting bills go to review, no silent merge or automatic/manual fixture receipt. Previously posted receipt immutable; no second receipt or silent unlink.
- BANK-DUP-04: Review includes Vietnamese labels and peer IDs in projection; duplicate rows excluded from provisional open-amount sum and counted separately. SHA-256 helper and 9x8 grayscale dHash helper do not decode images or detect crop regions; real examples remain future work.
- BANK-DUP-05: Static fixture tests cover exact repeat, possible recapture, disjoint regions, distinct reference, post-before-repeat, manual/auto block, replay, immutable fields and invalid bounds. Research ImageHash and TigerBeetle. No production or keyboard change.

## 106. BANK-IMAGE — v13.84 (phase 3j)
- BANK-IMAGE-01: Local PNG/JPEG/WebP decoding, max 16 MB and 16 MP, orientation from decoder, normalized raster max 1024 px; hash original bytes before resizing. No upload or storage. Unsupported input fails closed. Pixel limit checked after browser decoding.
- BANK-IMAGE-02: Horizontal whitespace projection proposes up to four regions; no assumption that a region is a real bill. Full-image fallback, original normalized coordinates, explicit user rotation of fingerprint sampling. No automatic crop recovery or perspective correction.
- BANK-IMAGE-03: Contrast and edge-detail signals flag low contrast, low detail, small region; thresholds experimental, never authorize OCR or payment. All regions remain unconfirmed and quality review. No trained blur or bill detector.
- BANK-IMAGE-04: Isolated ?billimagelab=1 module, local file chooser, region preview, whole-image and split buttons, rotation of hash only. No journal or production action. Superseded reads ignored; release bitmap. No new dependencies.
- BANK-IMAGE-05: Static raster/region/rotation/bounds tests and Chromium generated PNG test. References OpenCV contour segmentation, ImageHash luminance hash, Tesseract.js OCR worker interface. OCR adapter, real bill corpus and manual region editing remain separate. Preserve keyboard v13.27.

## 107. BANK-OCR — v13.85 (phase 3k)
- BANK-OCR-01: Parse one OCR text region, preserve line provenance and original values; explicit labels or a single following value line. Do not infer unlabelled clock, account, amount, name or reference. Unclassified lines kept for review. No engine in this phase.
- BANK-OCR-02: Parse money with explicit existing ParseAPI profile (vi-VN, agribank-en or canonical); require positive exact decimal, never substitute balances, fees or totals. Recipient STK must be digits, no silent O-to-0 fix. Names optional, preserve accents.
- BANK-OCR-03: Use existing ParseAPI.billDate with four-digit or explicit Vietnamese year; transaction-only clocks, never use send time or device time. Missing/invalid/conflicting fields remain null; confidence below experimental 80 flags review, confidence alone never authorizes posting.
- BANK-OCR-04: Text lab ?billocrlab=1 only; display extracted fields, line numbers and gaps. All records quality review and canPost false; no automatic evidence dispatch, actual OCR, payment or message. Multi-line memo beyond one labelled value and ambiguous templates remain unclassified.
- BANK-OCR-05: Static and browser fixtures include text date, exact fractional Bank amount, misleading balance/send time, invalid dates, conflicting amounts, recipient ID and OCR confidence. Consult Tesseract.js/PaddleOCR. Preserve keyboard v13.27.

## 108. BANK-ENGINE — v13.86 (phase 3l)
- BANK-ENGINE-01: Explicit demo-only Tesseract.js 6.0.1 loader, worker/core versions pinned, Vietnamese+English, no eager loading, no customer images; CDN language defaults are upstream-managed. No self-hosted verified assets yet. External scripts have page privileges: production/private data use is not approved.
- BANK-ENGINE-02: One worker per recognition session, sequential region reads, max eight regions; close once on completion/cancel, reject concurrent and closed calls, ignore stale results. Worker initialization may continue until it resolves; cancel is not a guaranteed immediate abort of download.
- BANK-ENGINE-03: Crop and rotate preview raster using proposed normalized source regions, retain source identity and OCR confidence, route raw text to BillOCRFieldAPI. No claim of full resolution OCR, automatic image segmentation, or calibrated accuracy.
- BANK-ENGINE-04: ?billenginelab=1 is isolated synthetic-image experiment, explicitly click to load CDN. All output review-only, no journal, uploads by application, messages or financial writes. No real customer bill until approved self-hosted asset path. No persistent OCR text cache.
- BANK-ENGINE-05: Static engine config and injected-worker Chromium lifecycle tests; real network/model accuracy not verified by those tests. Reference Tesseract.js v6.0.1 API/source. Preserve v13.27 keyboard.

## 109. BANK-CORPUS — v13.86 regression supplement
- BANK-CORPUS-01: Existing private CHAT receipt reason categories inform synthetic regression cases: missing printed time, recipient account conflict, multiple transactions, direction ambiguity, invalid date, misleading balance/fee and non-bill. No customer information or images enter public repo. Stored OCR/ledger labels are reference candidates, not independent image ground truth. Preserve raw VND vs ledger thousand-unit distinction. No production replay or write.

## 110. BANK-TEN — v13.87
- BANK-TEN-01: Ten independent fields align with CHAT BANK-317: sender bank, recipient bank/name/account, amount, printed date/time, memo, reference and trace. No inference or copying reference to trace. Missing values remain null.
- BANK-TEN-02: Printed date/time variants include YYYY/MM/DD, literal Vietnamese date without ngày, h and giờ clock, minute/second precision. Existing ParseAPI validates date; no assumed current year or send clock.
- BANK-TEN-03: fromStored accepts explicit ten-field structured objects for local review; never uses normalized database timestamps instead of printed date/time; rejects multiline field injection and unsafe amounts. Does not fetch storage or post.
- BANK-TEN-04: Keep existing OCR review gates and line evidence. The two lab views display all ten fields. No private bill data, names, accounts or images committed. Existing private CHAT rule casebook is a reference, not a data export.
- BANK-TEN-05: Regression covers independent bank/trace fields, missing clock, conflicting clock, alternate printed format and absent trace. No claim of OCR image accuracy or production acceptance. Preserve keyboard v13.27.

## 111. BANK-FORMAT — v13.88
- BANK-FORMAT-01: Recipient account groups may be separated only by ordinary/nonbreaking spaces; remove spaces, preserve every digit and leading zero. Reject letters, masking, punctuation and out-of-range lengths. No account completion or inferred digits.
- BANK-FORMAT-02: Shared ParseAPI accepts explicit abbreviated Vietnamese day/month/year (thg, Th, optional period, with/without comma); validate actual calendar and explicit four-digit year. No inferred dates or swapped day/month.
- BANK-FORMAT-03: Numeric transaction reference with one leading # may drop the marker for comparison; retain raw evidence; never copy it to trace. No broad punctuation cleanup.
- BANK-FORMAT-04: Read-only private CHAT structured metadata informed formats, no private values in public repo. No financial authorization or old record rewrite.
- BANK-FORMAT-05: Test grouped/leading-zero accounts, abbreviation variants, invalid calendar, missing year, masked account, raw reference and canonical equivalence. Preserve keyboard v13.27.

## 112. BANK-EVIDENCE — v13.89
- BANK-EVIDENCE-01: Explicit masked recipient account may expose a five-digit suffix and optional visible prefix; preserve raw evidence, never reconstruct full STK. Contradicting masks or full account conflict fail closed. Missing/partial account is not full proof.
- BANK-EVIDENCE-02: Reconcile only verified credit to configured full bank account and exact amount. Explicit partial evidence is separate, mismatched prefix/suffix blocks matching; compatible partial evidence remains review, never automatic receipt or automatic manual link.
- BANK-EVIDENCE-03: Memo equality or containment must use complete normalized tokens, not arbitrary substring; generic transfer text alone is insufficient. No fuzzy OCR correction. Existing amount/clock, reference/name conflict, duplicate and competing credit gates retained.
- BANK-EVIDENCE-04: Candidate exposes account, memo, reference and time proof independently; date-only same day remains review, late arrival flag compares transaction date to receipt day, no fabricated time. Recheck events preserve partial fields. No production write or old bill repair.
- BANK-EVIDENCE-05: Tests cover masked/contradicting account, partial candidate, missing clock, late bank, wrong suffix, substring collision, generic memo and full proof regression. Consult CHAT bill-app-preview and bill-transfer-identity; do not copy customer-specific constants. Preserve keyboard v13.27.

## 113. BANK-MEMO — v13.90
- BANK-MEMO-01: Keep original memo unchanged. Compare case, Vietnamese accents, punctuation and whitespace separately. Full token containment stays bounded; compact equality/containment with >=16 characters is review-only, never sufficient for fixture posting.
- BANK-MEMO-02: Anchored bank-side QR/IBFT prefix and complete MB(six digits)(memo) envelope can be separated; unknown wrapper not guessed. Numeric sequence conflicts remain review. Generic transfer phrase is insufficient.
- BANK-MEMO-03: Independently printed bill reference (>=8 chars, >=6 consecutive digits) must be delimited in bank memo. Strip only exact known trailing reference, preserve embedded reference and marker evidence. Never infer a reference from arbitrary digits; distinct explicit references block.
- BANK-MEMO-04: Raw memo preserved in bank-event identity, changing bank memo on same ID quarantines conflict. Full bank account, amount, valid transaction time, clear image, unique candidate and duplicate/ledger guards unchanged. Missing time stays review.
- BANK-MEMO-05: Test punctuation/accents/spacing, anchored wrappers, attached reference, substring collision, number conflict, generic phrase and late bank; source CHAT bank-app-preview, no private constants. Preserve v13.27 keyboard. No production write.

## 114. BANK-ACCOUNT — v13.91
- BANK-ACCOUNT-01: ParseAPI.accountDigits owns string account normalization. Remove horizontal presentation spaces (ordinary, NBSP, narrow NBSP, thin space, tab), preserve all digits and leading zeros. Reject numbers, newlines, punctuation, masks and letters.
- BANK-ACCOUNT-02: Full account 6–32 digits, explicit suffix exactly five digits, prefix 1–20. Four-digit examples show formatting only. Never pad or invent missing digits.
- BANK-ACCOUNT-03: OCR, configured destination, bank account, duplicate reference, manual fixture and journal use same canonical comparison. Partial evidence remains review. Preserve raw OCR evidence.
- BANK-ACCOUNT-04: Canonicalize account presentation in bank ID fingerprint; other conflicts unchanged. No production or historic receipt changes.
- BANK-ACCOUNT-05: Regression covers grouped full/suffix, leading zeros, wrong digits, unsupported symbols, duplicate and journal. Reviewed CHAT bill-amount-rules.mjs; stricter than broad non-digit removal. Preserve keyboard v13.27.

## 115. BANK-BATCH — v13.92
- BANK-BATCH-01: Distinct explicit references may exclude a cross-pair only when both sources explicitly declare the same shared-transfer reference namespace, both references are strong (>=8 chars, >=6 consecutive digits), bill is clear, full account matches and both clocks exist. No namespace inference from OCR or arbitrary bank routing codes. Embedded contrary evidence blocks exclusion.
- BANK-BATCH-02: Keep excluded cross-pairs separately for audit; count every remaining plausible pair including incomplete evidence. Unique bill and bank required; no greedy assignment or amount-only match. Unmatched third bill remains pending when all cross-pairs are proven distinct.
- BANK-BATCH-03: Missing/weak reference, missing namespace, unclear image or conflicting evidence stays competing. Duplicate bill/reference/image and consumed bank protections remain. No bank reuse.
- BANK-BATCH-04: Recheck carries referenceScope and bank fingerprint protects changes; fixture journal supports out-of-order late arrivals. Real bank namespace verification and production adapter remain unimplemented.
- BANK-BATCH-05: Regression covers three bills/two bank credits, reverse order, missing reference/scope, blurry competitor, duplicate credits, consumed IDs and late third credit. CHAT bill-transfer-identity reference; no private records or financial writes. Preserve v13.27.

## 116. BANK-NOTICE — v13.93
- BANK-NOTICE-01: Literal anchored OTT parser extracts printed day/clock, destination, sign, exact VND amount, memo, final balance and optional overdraft interest/debt. Explicit Agribank number profile; never substitute balance.
- BANK-NOTICE-02: Preserve raw text; classify QR, IBFT, MB, MBVCB, MCC, routing12 or other. Classification alone never validates a shared reference. Unknown format/repeated labels fail closed.
- BANK-NOTICE-03: Validate calendar, account and amount. Compare supplied stored amount/account/time, separate arrival and printed transaction dates. Own-transfer flag requires independent metadata.
- BANK-NOTICE-04: Always verified=false, referenceScope=null, canPost=false. No authenticated source adapter, production write or customer notice.
- BANK-NOTICE-05: Test decimal remainder, grouped STK, late arrival, debit, own transfer, conflict, invalid calendar/amount, ambiguous labels and overdraft. CHAT bill-app-preview reference and aggregate private format analysis. Preserve keyboard v13.27.

## 117. BANK-ENVELOPE — v13.94
- BANK-ENVELOPE-01: BankMemoEnvelopeAPI separates known anchored QR, IBFT, MB, MBVCB, MCC and routing12 envelopes; retain literal source and technical fields independently. Unknown/unsupported structures remain intact.
- BANK-ENVELOPE-02: MBVCB destination must match independent account; MCC embedded clock must be valid and within one minute of printed bank time. Conflicting or unknown context cannot supply wrapper proof.
- BANK-ENVELOPE-03: Wrapper evidence requires exact normalized principal and independently supplied full sender bound to bill memo, with no conflicting printed reference. Routing references never automatically become shared transfer IDs. No fuzzy correction.
- BANK-ENVELOPE-04: Exposes diagnostic review-only result, never changes existing matching/posting policy. No raw private records committed, no old receipt modification.
- BANK-ENVELOPE-05: Regression covers valid/invalid wrappers, destination/clock/reference conflict, missing sender, generic memo, unknown routing data. Source CHAT bill-app-preview.mjs and aggregate private notification formats. Preserve keyboard v13.27.

## 118. BANK-MCC-SENDER — v13.95
- BANK-MCC-SENDER-01: MCC supports observed optional anchored 10-character routing prefix (four digits, one letter, five digits) and source identifier 7–20 digits. A short source identifier is not a full account. Preserve technical fields; no arbitrary prefix stripping.
- BANK-MCC-SENDER-02: Require valid embedded clock within one minute of independent printed bank time; unsupported or conflicting wrapper retains original principal. Never infer a shared transfer reference.
- BANK-MCC-SENDER-03: Generic bill memo may expose separate senderMemoProof only if independently read full sender (>=3 words, >=12 characters), distinct known recipient, exact bank principal sender + same generic phrase, QR/plain format and no conflicts. Missing name or generic phrase alone fails.
- BANK-MCC-SENDER-04: Compact literal equality >=8 characters exposes diagnostic evidence only, never changes existing eligibility. Preserve original text/digits. All outputs canPost=false. No real financial decision, OCR rewrite, or historic bill change.
- BANK-MCC-SENDER-05: Test observed MCC variants, wrong prefix/clock, sender exact/mismatch/self/unknown recipient, compact equality and digit conflict. Private aggregate format analysis and CHAT bill-app-preview sender rule; no customer values committed. Preserve keyboard v13.27.

## 119. BANK-RESIDUAL — v13.96
- BANK-RESIDUAL-01: Preserve literal memo on both sides. Diagnostic compact comparison reports missing, short equality, digit conflict, single contiguous gap, one-letter difference, containment or different. No inferred correction; digit conflict takes priority when both sides exist.
- BANK-RESIDUAL-02: Gap/letter difference and short equality are review hints only, never equivalent to full memo, sender or reference proof. No automatic eligibility or posting change. Do not treat repeated candidate pairs as unique receipts.
- BANK-RESIDUAL-03: MCC exposes signed delta seconds and same Vietnam calendar day flag; retain clock conflict when absolute delta >60 seconds. Do not infer offset or alternate day, or replace printed bank timestamp. Conflicting wrapper remains original.
- BANK-RESIDUAL-04: Diagnostics expose reason and counts, no private raw payload in source. No historical or production record changes; no automatic financial action.
- BANK-RESIDUAL-05: Tests cover contiguous missing span, one letter, digit difference, short equality, containment, missing and cross-day MCC conflict. CHAT verifiedMbvcbTransferSuffixOcr guard reviewed; no broad fuzzy matching. Preserve keyboard v13.27.

## 120. BANK-NOCLOCK — v13.97
- BANK-NOCLOCK-01: Distinguish truly absent printed bill clock from invalid/conflicting clock. Require explicit valid bill date, same printed bank day, valid bank clock, clear image, full recipient account/name, exact positive amount, verified incoming bank and independently declared non-own-transfer evidence.
- BANK-NOCLOCK-02: Require strong alphanumeric bill reference (>=8 chars and >=6 consecutive digits) delimited in BOTH literal memos and exact normalized full memo. No conflicting reference, digit, name, date or receipt time. Only missing clock may remain as candidate reason.
- BANK-NOCLOCK-03: Require unique bill and bank candidate, no duplicate or consumed evidence. Expose missing-clock-reference verification with billTime=null, separately printed bank clock/date and arrival date. Late arrival never fills missing OCR time. Review only; canPost=false; no receipt or manual link authorization.
- BANK-NOCLOCK-04: Queue shows separate proof and next action, retains amount in outstanding total. Recheck persists ownTransfer evidence; explicitly own transfers excluded. Unknown own-transfer cannot qualify this proof. No actual messages, DB writes or historical corrections.
- BANK-NOCLOCK-05: Test late next-day arrival, invalid clock, absent name, wrong account/reference, generic/missing memo, own transfer, ambiguity, replay and blocked posting. CHAT previewBillReferenceMatch reference. Preserve keyboard v13.27.

## 121. BANK-CLOCK — v13.98
- BANK-CLOCK-01: Bill clock is mandatory for automatic collection. Missing, invalid or contradictory printed clock enters mandatory review immediately, even before Bank arrives. A valid explicit clock in the printed date-time field counts; upload, notification and device clocks never substitute.
- BANK-CLOCK-02: Same amount, account, date, memo, reference and later Bank evidence cannot clear missing clock; keep v13.97 evidence review-only. Only a new verified printed clock revision can restore normal automatic candidate evaluation. Explicit historical manual entries remain separate, never silently changed.
- BANK-CLOCK-03: Queue exposes clockState, clockReviewRequired, count and customer verification draft asking for hour:minute or complete bill image. Draft only, no notification delivery, no real customer message.
- BANK-CLOCK-04: Day rollover/reconnect/Bank arrival never clear mandatory review; preserve original clock and evidence. No financial writes, historic receipt edits or production deployment.
- BANK-CLOCK-05: Tests cover no Bank, blank/invalid/conflicting clock, explicit date-time, late Bank, recheck and corrected OCR. Preserve keyboard v13.27.

## 122. BANK-RELATED — v13.98 revision 2
- BANK-RELATED-01: Missing printed clock remains mandatory review. Read-only related candidates require same valid Vietnam date, exact positive amount, full receiving account, clear bill, verified incoming Bank and explicit non-own-transfer; no date, account, name, reference, digit or arrival-time conflict.
- BANK-RELATED-02: Exact substantive memo with matching recipient name is priority; contained/compact substantive memo or bounded literal residual with matching name is possible only. No similarity score authorizes collection; generic text alone is insufficient.
- BANK-RELATED-03: Expose candidate Bank ID, separately printed Bank clock, date, late arrival, competing evidence and canPost=false. Multiple candidates remain competing; no automatic selection or link. Duplicate and posted rows excluded from projection.
- BANK-RELATED-04: Recompute on Bank arrival and OCR correction via existing owners. No actual messages, production deployment or financial changes.
- BANK-RELATED-05: Regression for blank clock, missing Bank, priority, residual, competing, own transfer, conflicting evidence and OCR correction. Browser asserts initial 2 pending + 1 review and candidate rendering; preserve keyboard v13.27.

## 123. BANK-FIELDS — v13.99
- BANK-FIELDS-01: Related candidate evidence is a read-only projection per bank ID and bill ID; seven fields (amount, account, recipient, date, clock, memo, reference). Keep literal account/name/memo/reference, canonical exact amount/date; null remains unknown. Never fill bill clock from Bank.
- BANK-FIELDS-02: Distinguish exact, normalized memo, containment, spacing, residual, embedded and unconfirmed proof. Missing reference is not a mismatch or a match. No inferred correction or new eligibility.
- BANK-FIELDS-03: Each competing candidate gets its own comparison card; no default candidate date/time at bill level when multiple exist. Reuse CardController and FormatAPI. No auto-select or receipt authority.
- BANK-FIELDS-04: Recompute from current OCR revision and Bank evidence; clear related projection on corrected clock. Preserve prior rules, keyboard baseline and historical receipts. No production writes or actual messages.
- BANK-FIELDS-05: Test original values, null clock, one-letter residual, multiple candidates, ordering, corrected OCR and browser render/removal. Full CI required.

## 124. BANK-COMPETE — v13.100
- BANK-COMPETE-01: Build connected bill/Bank candidate groups seeded by related missing-clock bills; include every existing non-excluded competing edge for open bills, even lower-evidence contenders. Never change matching or receipt eligibility.
- BANK-COMPETE-02: Count unique bill IDs and unique Bank record IDs separately; repeated same-ID notification is counted once. Distinct record IDs are not proven distinct financial transfers. Never add candidate edges as received money.
- BANK-COMPETE-03: Display separate face-value total of bills and sum of related Bank records only when all values in that group are known; not a debt, amount collected, or confirmed credit total. No auto-assign or auto-link. Posted records remain excluded.
- BANK-COMPETE-04: Stable sorted group IDs and shared Bank IDs; recompute from existing snapshot on changes. Use CardController and MoneyAPI; no production actions or history edits.
- BANK-COMPETE-05: Tests 3 bills/1 record, replay, 2 records, competing clocked bill, posted exclusion, ordering, UI group; preserve keyboard v13.27 and CI gate.

## 125. BANK-REPLAY — v13.101
- BANK-REPLAY-01: Cross-ID replay diagnostic requires verified incoming official Agribank package, explicit non-own-transfer, full configured account, exact positive amount, valid printed clock, exact literal entire raw notice including balance and matching stored memo. Reuse AgribankNoticeAPI; receipt timestamp may differ, never identity.
- BANK-REPLAY-02: Equal amount/minute alone, changed balance/body, missing source or contradictory fields never prove replay. Same ID conflict remains quarantined; preserve raw evidence and all record IDs.
- BANK-REPLAY-03: Unposted suspected replay group remains review-only with original competing candidates. If one exact notice alias is already posted, block all its validated aliases from candidate evaluation; no automatic link or second receipt. Do not infer identity from approximate text.
- BANK-REPLAY-04: Show unique record IDs, one notice face amount and replay warning; suppress group Bank sum when replay evidence exists. No changes to production, historical data, or delivery.
- BANK-REPLAY-05: Regression covers different receipt days, changed balance, changed memo/clock/account/amount/source, absent raw, posted alias, review projection and browser. Keep keyboard v13.27; full CI gate.

## 126. BANK-CROP — v13.102
- BANK-CROP-01: Retain exact SHA and original region identity; add bounded 5/10/15% symmetric inset dHash variants from the current raster, with same chosen rotation. No image upload or inference of missing pixels. Store at most three unique hashes in immutable cropHashes.
- BANK-CROP-02: New crop hint requires distinct source hashes, full matching account, exact amount, same printed day, substantive equal normalized memo (>=12 characters), no contradictory known name, distinct reference or known clock difference >60 seconds. Non-informative all/mostly flat dHashes excluded from new crop hint. One full-to-inset distance <=3 is experimental review evidence only.
- BANK-CROP-03: Known clock difference >60 seconds also suppresses existing near-image hint, but never exact overlapping source or same explicit reference conflict. No automatic merge or receipt; preserve previous posted ledger and all bill IDs. Missing time remains mandatory review.
- BANK-CROP-04: Display crop fingerprints in existing local intake. Limits: symmetric modest border crop only, no perspective or arbitrary recapture guarantee; synthetic fixture is not real-bank accuracy. No production data or history edits.
- BANK-CROP-05: Test synthetic 10% crop, independent fields, conflicting clock/reference, invalid hash, review guard and browser label. Preserve keyboard v13.27; CI required.

## 127. BANK-REGION — v13.103
- BANK-REGION-01: Extend BANK-IMAGE-02 with row and column whitespace projection, bounded at four auto-proposed regions; grid when both axes have accepted gaps and product <=4. Otherwise retain one axis. Explicit row/column/full controls; never assume proposal count is transaction count.
- BANK-REGION-02: Region plan identity = original SHA-256 + normalized rectangle. Unique keys, valid known selection, stable under reorder; one source can contain multiple regions. Distinct sources remain separate, not automatically merged.
- BANK-REGION-03: Reject repeated identical region keys; flag overlapping rectangles of same source and block their read-plan eligibility. Selected distinct regions only mark requested OCR scope, not confirmed OCR, transfer or receipt. No payment authority.
- BANK-REGION-04: Reset region selection on new image, split mode or rotation. Existing local image module only, no actual OCR job, upload, journal or production changes. Whitespace projection cannot reliably segment touching/overlapping/complex collage; manual geometry editing remains future work.
- BANK-REGION-05: Test synthetic 2x2 grid, row/column modes, stable selection, overlap, duplicate and unknown keys, distinct sources, browser selection/reset. Preserve keyboard v13.27 and CI gate.

## 128. BANK-REGION-OCR — v13.104
- BANK-REGION-OCR-01: Validate selected stable region keys with existing regionPlan before opening OCR worker; reject overlaps, empty or unknown selection. Only selected crops reach engine, no full-image fallback.
- BANK-REGION-OCR-02: Sequential per-region recognition/parse, original region identity, raw text and confidence kept separately; never fill missing fields from neighbors. All outputs review-only and canPost=false.
- BANK-REGION-OCR-03: Failed region yields error with null parsed/text/confidence; close failed session and start fresh for later regions. Cancellation or changed source/selection invalidates stale outputs. No automated retry or posting.
- BANK-REGION-OCR-04: Existing billenginelab sample-only CDN adapter uses explicit region selection. No private image processing claim, production deployment, journal, real messages or historic repair.
- BANK-REGION-OCR-05: Test three mock regions with middle failure and missing last clock; assert unique identities, exact independent amounts and lifecycle. Static and browser checks required; real engine accuracy not claimed. Preserve keyboard v13.27.

## 129. BANK-FIELD-REVISION — v13.105
- BANK-FIELD-REVISION-01: Create per-region field review from existing parser with explicit money profile; ten fields start unverified/missing/conflict. Never infer verified from OCR confidence. Keep original raw evidence and per-field lines.
- BANK-FIELD-REVISION-02: Reread accepts one explicitly selected field and same region key + expected revision; other fields from returned text never overwrite existing values. Keep proposal separate until explicit confirmation. Missing/conflicting proposals cannot be confirmed.
- BANK-FIELD-REVISION-03: Confirm only a valid literal candidate with evidence. Lock verified field against reread or further confirmation in current review session. Reject stale/wrong region, unknown field; bounded immutable history. No automatic ledger authorization or overwrite of historic receipt.
- BANK-FIELD-REVISION-04: Text-only sample lab exposes reread and explicit simulated confirmation; physical field crop requires independent coordinates and remains future work. No actual OCR model or customer images, no journal, messages, production changes.
- BANK-FIELD-REVISION-05: Test isolated time reread, unrelated fields unchanged, verified lock, stale/wrong region, missing proposal, immutable history and browser confirmation. Keep keyboard v13.27; full CI required.

## 130. BANK-FIELD-GEOMETRY — v13.106
- BANK-FIELD-GEOMETRY-01: Request Tesseract blocks explicitly, preserve same-region rotated OCR canvas dimensions. Map nonblank text lines to block/paragraph/line output only when normalized full ordered sequence matches exactly; otherwise all field bounds unknown. Never guess line alignment.
- BANK-FIELD-GEOMETRY-02: Derive bounds only from one valid candidate with label/value line provenance, union both lines when needed. Coordinates local to rotated crop (not original source). Conflicts, missing fields, invalid bounds or ambiguous lines require manual selection. Line bounds are not exact word bounds.
- BANK-FIELD-GEOMETRY-03: fieldCropPlan validates source SHA+region key, rotation, canvas size, field and bounded integer rectangle; no cross-bill or full-image fallback. Explicit manual rectangle allowed. cropField copies only that rectangle. Geometry is review evidence, not proof of transaction or posting.
- BANK-FIELD-GEOMETRY-04: Existing sample engine lab shows line bounds or manual-required; no interactive manual picker or automatic field reread in this phase. No real bill processing, journal, production writes or messages.
- BANK-FIELD-GEOMETRY-05: Direct tests for blank-line alignment, next-line values, rotated coordinates, mismatch, missing field, manual rectangle and invalid/cross-source geometry; browser mock block response and pixel crop. Preserve keyboard v13.27 and CI gate.

## 131. BANK-FIELD-VARIANTS — v13.107
- BANK-FIELD-VARIANTS-01: Explicit same-source field crop only, validate decoded SHA/normalized region and field geometry. Three bounded independent inputs: original crop, grayscale contrast stretch (min spread 24), 2x scale (max 2048 dimension). Never modify source raster or infer missing pixels.
- BANK-FIELD-VARIANTS-02: Sequential worker calls, preserve raw result and confidence per method. Compare only selected field using existing literal parser. No confidence ranking, fuzzy correction or majority vote. Agreement of all three produces review proposal only; mismatch/conflicting field blocks proposal; missing/error leaves incomplete review.
- BANK-FIELD-VARIANTS-03: No automatic confirmation, no field overwrite, no financial authority. Existing verified lock remains. Failed worker is closed before next variant; cancel and stale results guarded.
- BANK-FIELD-VARIANTS-04: Sample CDN engine lab exposes explicit field selection and rerun only for known bounds. Manual rectangle API exists but interactive picker not yet implemented. No AI model, private bill or production use; no uploads by application or historic changes.
- BANK-FIELD-VARIANTS-05: Direct comparison, contrast pixel and wrong-scope regressions; mock browser crop sizes, conflict, source pixel and close lifecycle. Preserve keyboard v13.27; full CI required. Thresholds experimental, no real OCR accuracy claim.

## 132. BANK-LITERAL — v13.108
- BANK-LITERAL-01: Preserve invalid labeled raw OCR value, line and labelLine. Diagnostic positions are zero-based Unicode code points relative to trimmed OCR field, not image coordinates. Possible glyph groups 0/O, 1/I/l/|, 2/Z, 5/S, 6/G, 8/B only warn on invalid numeric field; no substitution or suggested repaired value.
- BANK-LITERAL-02: Retain explicit money profile and existing exact ParseAPI; valid separators and account horizontal presentation spaces are informational. Invalid punctuation/space remains review, no broad digit stripping. Leading zeros unchanged; dates with valid words unchanged.
- BANK-LITERAL-03: Valid alphanumeric reference/trace, names and memo are not suspicious simply for letters. Compare actual differing field raw values between OCR methods; label glyph, separator or length/spacing difference, preserve canonical equality flag. No fuzzy correction, score override or eligibility change.
- BANK-LITERAL-04: Existing OCR text lab displays raw invalid value and position; variant UI shows differing raw values. Review only, no real OCR accuracy claim, production action or history rewrite.
- BANK-LITERAL-05: Test O/I numeric errors, grouped account/leading zeros, profile punctuation, valid names/reference, cross-method confusable reference, separator/space differences, browser raw display. Preserve keyboard v13.27 and CI gate.

## 133. BANK-MEMO-CODE — v13.109
- BANK-MEMO-CODE-01: Distinguish literal/case/accent/whitespace/punctuation/compact memo presentation; retain raw source. Compare numeric-bearing tokens after only recognized wrapper/reference handling, preserve punctuation within tokens and leading zeros. Token grammar is a conservative diagnostic, not bank reference extraction.
- BANK-MEMO-CODE-02: If otherwise matched memo has unequal numeric-bearing tokens, including one-sided unverified codes or altered punctuation, require memo-code-review; never silently discard unknown suffix. Existing numeric conflicts retain priority. No relaxed eligibility.
- BANK-MEMO-CODE-03: Independently printed strong reference must not be a substring joined to an alphanumeric token by dot/underscore/slash/hyphen. Delimited reference handling remains, known wrapper handling unchanged. Do not treat a routing code as transfer identity.
- BANK-MEMO-CODE-04: Candidate evidence and existing review UI retain both token lists, explain the reason; exclude code-conflict candidates from missing-clock related suggestions, not from review queue. No production data, history or real receipts.
- BANK-MEMO-CODE-05: Test accents, punctuation in codes, one-sided unknown code, exact delimited reference, attached compound reference, missing-clock guard. Preserve keyboard v13.27 and CI gate.

## 134. BANK-MEMO-SPAN — v13.110
- BANK-MEMO-SPAN-01: Extend existing residual diagnostic with immutable normalized compact spans (match, bill-only, bank-only, different). Show ordered unique containment or single-gap and single-letter evidence; do not infer which source lost text or reconstruct original memo.
- BANK-MEMO-SPAN-02: Repeated containment or ambiguous gap placement returns ambiguous-position without fabricated alignment. Missing, short, digit-conflict and unrelated inputs produce no aligned spans. Preserve original raw evidence separately.
- BANK-MEMO-SPAN-03: Keep all previous financial gates; for missing-clock residual suggestions require unique alignment and no numeric token codeReview even when memo kind is none. No expanded matching or posting authorization.
- BANK-MEMO-SPAN-04: Expose diagnostic per candidate in existing review evidence and related cards; normalized spans are not original image coordinates and cannot prove bank truncation. No real records, messages or historical repair.
- BANK-MEMO-SPAN-05: Test both containment directions, internal gap, one-letter, repeated substring, digit/code conflict, immutable spans and review-only related candidate. Preserve keyboard v13.27; full CI required.

## 135. BANK-PAYER — v13.111
- BANK-PAYER-01: Extend OCR field set from ten to eleven with explicit senderName; legacy name remains recipient only. Only explicit sender labels or stored sender_name accepted. No name inference from memo or sender account. Preserve literal evidence and parser review-only output.
- BANK-PAYER-02: BankReconcileAPI.payerRoles separates bill/bank sender, bill/bank recipient, explicit customer ID/name and memo mentions. Same-name or memo mention is not a customer identity; differing names indicate possible third-party, not proof. All role diagnostics canAssignCustomer=false, canPost=false.
- BANK-PAYER-03: Known sender mismatch adds sender-name-conflict and blocks related suggestions. Explicit customer context requires separate allocation review even when names match; no new customer posting path. Legacy unscoped fixture matching remains unchanged. Bank duplicate ID fingerprint includes normalized sender.
- BANK-PAYER-04: Carry role evidence through recheck and review card. Existing customer context cannot be replaced or introduced by OCR revision; no real directory binding, customer receipt allocation, message or production action. Historical simulated journal is not a customer ledger.
- BANK-PAYER-05: Test labelled roles, stored adaptation, no memo inference, third-party/same-name review, sender conflict, missing-clock gate, OCR context lock and browser labels. Update ten-field historical contract count intentionally; preserve v13.27 keyboard and full CI gate.

## 136. BANK-ALLOC — v13.112
- BANK-ALLOC-01: BankJournalAPI.planAllocations is read-only, fixture-only; derive receipt amount from replayed bank-backed receipt, not a bill face value or supplied amount. One plan per receipt and bank ID; reject known replay alias across plans. Unlinked manual receipt is not Bank money.
- BANK-ALLOC-02: Explicit order ID, customer ID and outstanding revision required, independently provided obligation snapshot. Unique order within plan, cumulative remaining across plans, exact positive canonical decimal string amount; no allocation above current outstanding or receipt amount. No automatic division or inference from names/memo.
- BANK-ALLOC-03: Preserve exact received, allocated and unallocated totals using DecimalAPI; residual is not automatically customer credit. Count distinct bank records, orders per receipt and customer IDs separately; no claim of bank identity beyond existing replay evidence. All projections canPost=false; no journal mutation.
- BANK-ALLOC-04: New fixture lab uses existing Card/Input/Button, three obligations across two customers, editable proposed amounts; no live directory, order lookup, authorization or ledger adapter. Snapshot revision is local test evidence, not server concurrency protection.
- BANK-ALLOC-05: Test fractional residual, multi-order/multi-customer, two receipts against cumulative outstanding, duplicate receipt/order, stale or wrong customer, overpayment and negative amount, missing receipt. Preserve keyboard v13.27 and CI gate.

## 137. BANK-CYCLE — v13.113
- BANK-CYCLE-01: Reuse BankJournalAPI.planAllocations; group only explicitly provided customer/cycle ID/revision and order IDs. Require each obligation assigned once, correct customer, no duplicate cycle scope; do not infer cycles from dates or names. Complete flag is caller-supplied fixture scope, not verified server completeness.
- BANK-CYCLE-02: Sum exact opening outstanding, allocated and remaining per order and cycle; keep per-receipt contributions and distinct bank/receipt IDs. Counts per cycle are nonadditive across shared receipts; global counts stay in allocation result. Unallocated residual remains global, never customer credit.
- BANK-CYCLE-03: State unpaid/partial-plan/covered-plan/no-opening-debt/incomplete-scope; incomplete cannot claim covered even if zero. All results canPost=false and canCloseCycle=false. Pending/unlinked bill or manual receipt is not bank-backed allocation; no actual debt settlement or cycle reset.
- BANK-CYCLE-04: Existing allocation fixture lab extends to two receipts, editable amounts and cycle remaining/status. No live customer/obligation data, cycle cadence inference, messages or production adapter.
- BANK-CYCLE-05: Test partial, covered, two receipts to one order, shared receipt, fractional residual, incomplete scope, wrong customer, duplicate receipt and unassigned order; browser verifies ready/partial/rejected. Preserve keyboard v13.27 and full CI gate.

## 138. BANK-ROLLING — v13.113 correction (user-confirmed model)
- BANK-ROLLING-01: Main business model is rolling customer debt (nợ đuổi, thu gạch). Delivered purchases subtract, explicitly assigned verified/posted fixture receipts add to customer signed balance. No required invoice/order allocation; BANK-ALLOC and BANK-CYCLE remain optional isolated diagnostics, not main flow. Draft orders not counted.
- BANK-ROLLING-02: Explicit customer ID, opening balance, snapshot revision, period and assigned receipt IDs; no inference from names or memo. Replay journal for amount, count receipt once, linked manual+bank once, unlinked manual separately. Reject wrong known customer, repeated receipt/bank/known alias. Receipt assignment is fixture input, not authorization or global cross-customer lock.
- BANK-ROLLING-03: Use exact DecimalAPI and chronological effective posting timestamps, Vietnam business date; reject ambiguous same-time events within fixture. Negative balance is debt, zero clean, positive credit. Cycle stays open while negative; a transition to >=0 is a projected closure; subsequent negative starts new cycle. Unknown opening start stays unknown; incomplete scope cannot claim settlement. No actual closing, automatic reminder change or real posting.
- BANK-ROLLING-04: ?bankrollinglab=1 primary model demonstration; optional allocation lab is not mandatory. Do not rewrite production history or change existing debt rules/cadence; no production connector.
- BANK-ROLLING-05: Test purchases after opening, partial payment, zero, positive credit, subsequent purchase, incomplete scope, duplicate receipts and wrong customer/draft. Browser test debt/zero/credit; full CI gate. Preserve keyboard v13.27.

## 139. BANK-ROLLING-BATCH — v13.114
- BANK-ROLLING-BATCH-01: Restore unmerged v13.113 rolling correction into main-bound release; nợ đuổi/thu gạch is primary, optional invoice allocation is not required. Explicit batch scope, one customer snapshot per ID; no production change.
- BANK-ROLLING-BATCH-02: One receipt assignment per batch across all customers, explicit customer ID/revision, reject duplicate receipt/bank/known replay alias and duplicate purchase ID; no inferred assignment or split. Reuse single rolling projection and one replay; unassigned receipts remain separate. Assignment is proposed fixture input, not persistent/global lock across independent requests.
- BANK-ROLLING-BATCH-03: Manual receipt linked to Bank stays one receipt at original postedAt. As-of projection before verifiedAt counts it as unlinked manual, after link as Bank-backed; do not add second payment. Exact separate debt and credit totals, no offset across customers. Caller snapshot completeness only.
- BANK-ROLLING-BATCH-04: Existing rolling lab uses batch API; no live customer or ledger adapter, no changes to reminder cadence, history or actual settlement. Full journal integrity replay remains required; not an independently reconstructed historical ledger.
- BANK-ROLLING-BATCH-05: Test cross-customer duplicate, stale revision, unknown customer, unassigned receipt, manual then Bank link and earlier as-of classification, exact residual. Restore previous browser debt/zero/credit regression; CI gate and verify actual merged main blob. Preserve v13.27 keyboard.

## 140. BANK-ROLLING-PERSIST — v13.115
- BANK-ROLLING-PERSIST-01: Synthetic account 123456789 only; one stable account-keyed IndexedDB journal and receipt-owner registry, not scoped by day, tab or session. Existing independent journals are not imported.
- BANK-ROLLING-PERSIST-02: Atomic read-modify-write on one accounts store; identical evidence/receipt entry and same owner retry is a no-op; conflicting payload or customer rejected. No silent reassignment, no inference from name or amount.
- BANK-ROLLING-PERSIST-03: Replay whole journal before and after mutation, preserving original postedAt, exact amount, manual-to-bank linkage without second receipt; later known Bank aliases cannot be assigned twice. Transaction failure rolls back both evidence and ownership.
- BANK-ROLLING-PERSIST-04: Browser fixture API only at ?bankpersistlab=1; no real balance changes, no messages, no reminder changes, no production adapter. Local same-origin storage can be cleared or isolated by browser/PWA; server-side database uniqueness and posting transaction remain required.
- BANK-ROLLING-PERSIST-05: Direct checks for replay, unknown receipt, owner conflict, late manual link, synthetic-only scope; browser regression for abort, concurrent tabs, reload and reopen. Preserve keyboard v13.27; CI gate before release.

## 141. BANK-ROLLING-DB — v13.116
- BANK-ROLLING-DB-01: Standalone synthetic PostgreSQL fixture only, not a Supabase migration or production adapter. Account fixture:123456789 only; private schema, RLS enabled, no public/anon/authenticated/service_role grants, SECURITY INVOKER, fixed search_path.
- BANK-ROLLING-DB-02: Database uniqueness by account+receipt ID, account+bill ID and account+verified provider transaction identity. No day/session/recipient customer in the Bank identity; no deduplication on amount/date/memo alone. Stable canonical Bank identity must come from a trusted verification service; absent or ambiguous identity needs review. No OCR guess or notification ID as transaction identity.
- BANK-ROLLING-DB-03: Same receipt and immutable posting payload replay is a no-op; conflicting owner or payload rejects. Manual receipt late Bank link locks existing row, preserves original postedAt and amount; verifiedAt separately recorded. Missing bank transaction timestamp rejected. Trusted service must validate explicit clock from evidence before calling; SQL timestamp alone cannot prove clock was present.
- BANK-ROLLING-DB-04: Exact numeric amount, no rounding during ledger operations, atomic SQL transaction. No actual customer balance, notification, cycle closing or reminder changes. Different verified transaction IDs may share amount and timestamp. Manual copies with different bill IDs and missing canonical Bank ID still require evidence review; old independent journal data not imported.
- BANK-ROLLING-DB-05: CI-only PostgreSQL 16 service runs serial checks, rollback and two independent concurrent sessions. Existing browser/UI CI still required, preserve keyboard v13.27. No production rollout until authoritative identity, legacy reconciliation, auth and atomic actual ledger adapter are separately verified.

## 142. BANK-IDENTITY — v13.117
- BANK-IDENTITY-01: Separate notice ID, bill/OCR ID, memo routing token, trace and authoritative provider transaction ID. Only a distinct statement attestation may resolve the synthetic key; no inference from OCR or matching content.
- BANK-IDENTITY-02: Account fixture 123456789 only; validate raw Agribank credit notice, receiving account, exact amount, explicit time, source package, receivedAt and stored field agreement. Same provider ID and same exact statement transaction time/amount group aliases across receipt days and notice content changes. Preserve leading zeros and case; different verified IDs remain distinct.
- BANK-IDENTITY-03: Missing authoritative mapping, inconsistent evidence or competing mappings fail closed; conflicts invalidate affected keys, not just one notice. No assumption that Agribank publishes a stable provider ID in its notifications.
- BANK-IDENTITY-04: prepare calls existing bill match for a single candidate and rejects missing bill clock. Candidate only; full batch competition, customer assignment, persistent identity and database posting checks remain required. canPost=false; no production adapter, no real statement or authorization.
- BANK-IDENTITY-05: Ten direct cases and static regression for aliases, distinct IDs, no proof, amount conflict, ambiguity, missing bank/bill clock, exact case-sensitive key, candidate and no-post. Preserve v13.27 keyboard and existing CI database fixture.

## 143. BANK-IDENTITY-BATCH — v13.118
- BANK-IDENTITY-BATCH-01: Whole supplied fixture batch uses BankTransactionIdentityAPI and BankReconcileAPI; collapse edges only by explicitly resolved canonical Bank key, never by amount or date. Include unresolved candidates in competition; no day cutoff; reject future notice relative to explicit asOf.
- BANK-IDENTITY-BATCH-02: Require complete scope assertion and explicit synthetic customer assignment or prior receipt owner; no inference from payer/name/memo. Customer-allocation-review alone may be satisfied by explicit fixture assignment, never by matching name. Other evidence gaps, missing clock, duplicates and competing bills/keys block proposals.
- BANK-IDENTITY-BATCH-03: Prior receipt snapshot validates unique receipt/bill/canonical key, immutable owner and positive exact amount. Prior linked receipt remains collected; reserve used keys even if its original bill is absent. Manual unlinked receipt with exact matching amount proposes verification only, newCredit=0, original postedAt retained.
- BANK-IDENTITY-BATCH-04: Separate new-receipt-plan, manual-link-plan, already-collected, manual-await-bank, review and pending. Per-customer and global exact new credit and manual link totals; no actual posting, balance or cycle mutation. Complete flag is caller assertion, not authoritative database completeness; no cross-session lock integration.
- BANK-IDENTITY-BATCH-05: Regression for two customers across days, notice aliases, manual-link totals, competing bills, unresolved alias, missing clock, already-collected, prior key used elsewhere, incomplete scope and no owner; preserve v13.27 keyboard and existing CI. No real messages or data.

## 144. BANK-ACCUMULATION — v13.119
- BANK-ACCUMULATION-01: Synthetic account only, append-only evidence timeline, explicit event ID and ISO instant, nondecreasing event time. Exact replay is a no-op; changed payload for the same ID rejects. Vietnam business date derived from TimeAPI; no midnight purge.
- BANK-ACCUMULATION-02: Accept bill before or after Bank, OCR revisions in sequence, immutable customer/image context, immutable Bank notice ID and explicit statement evidence. Keep firstSeenAt and lastChangedAt separate. Day/reconnect/manual triggers re-evaluate without inventing evidence.
- BANK-ACCUMULATION-03: Explicit fixture customer assignment and receipt snapshot; no inference from name. Preserve prior receipt ID, customer, amount, method and postedAt; late manual Bank linkage preserves original credit and records verifiedAt separately. No actual receipt creation or historical mutation.
- BANK-ACCUMULATION-04: Every new evidence event invalidates completeness; explicit scope assertion with expected revision required before new proposals. Replay as of a historical cutoff excludes later evidence; entire journal is bounded to 400 events and not a persistent store. Existing BankIdentityBatchAPI remains the proposal owner. No production data, automatic sends or actual debt changes.
- BANK-ACCUMULATION-05: Regression for bill-first and Bank-first, late notice, missing clock, OCR correction, manual link, historical cutoff, retry/conflict, backwards time, revision conflict and completeness gate. Preserve keyboard v13.27; both CI workflows required.

## 145. BANK-BACKLOG — v13.120
- BANK-BACKLOG-01: Derive read-only queue from BankAccumulationAPI historical replay; preserve bill firstSeenAt and OCR lastChangedAt, age in elapsed hours and Vietnam calendar days. No midnight expiry, no new payment ledger.
- BANK-BACKLOG-02: Separate waiting-bank, review, ready-plan, manual-unverified and closed; missing clock explicitly prioritized even when scope incomplete. Manual receipt stays in verification queue until linked; closed rows retained but excluded from open counts.
- BANK-BACKLOG-03: Group by explicitly assigned customer, unknown owner separate; count open, manual, review, ready and waiting. No sum of unverified bill face amounts as debt. NewCreditPlan and manualLinkPlan separate, no actual posting.
- BANK-BACKLOG-04: Group Bank notices only by canonical attested identity; distinguish candidate, no-bill, already-collected and unresolved/conflicting notices. No-bill amount is sum of distinct resolved candidate-free keys in supplied scope, not actual unallocated balance; unresolved amounts not included. Reserve prior linked keys even when original bill absent. canPost=false, canSend=false.
- BANK-BACKLOG-05: Regression for 3-day aging, two customers, missing clock, waiting Bank, manual linkage, canonical aliases, no-bill Bank, unresolved identity, exact separate amounts and historical cutoff. No persistent adapter, messages, reminders, production data or keyboard changes; full CI required.

## 146. BANK-BACKLOG-UI — v13.121
- BANK-BACKLOG-UI-01: Bill module includes read-only synthetic backlog, also available via bankbackloglab=1; use existing Layout, Typography, Button and Card owners. No separate UI CSS system or keyboard edits.
- BANK-BACKLOG-UI-02: Filters for open, missing clock, manual unverified, Bank, closed. Display counts, customer, exact source-derived status, waiting days and next action; separate new-credit plan from manual verification and candidate-free Bank totals. No inference from bill face amounts into debt.
- BANK-BACKLOG-UI-03: Details expand on demand, aria-expanded/controls, pressed filter state and status announcement. Show distinct bill arrival, OCR revision time, Bank transaction and arrival time, canonical identity and notice aliases. No fake real financial action.
- BANK-BACKLOG-UI-04: Explicitly labeled fixture-only manual verification uses BankAccumulationAPI snapshot and scope revision, zero additional credit. Reset returns to synthetic initial state; no message transport, durable storage or production writes. Keep existing Bill cards and keyboard v13.27.
- BANK-BACKLOG-UI-05: Browser regression at 320/390/1280 for filters, details, manual linkage, zero credit, reset, narrow width and no page errors. Existing application and PostgreSQL CI required before merge.

## 147. BANK-FIELD-COMPARE — v13.122
- BANK-FIELD-COMPARE-01: BankFieldCompareAPI.compare and project are read-only synthetic diagnostics; preserve literal Bill and Bank values. Compare exact decimal amount, normalized receiving account, recipient/sender separately, transaction date, clock, memo, reference, canonical identity and source. Never use field counts as posting authority.
- BANK-FIELD-COMPARE-02: Explicit statuses match, near, missing, conflict, review. Clock difference up to 60 seconds is near; invalid/missing clock remains review, never filled from Bank. Keep original memo and flag numeric/code/spacing conflicts; do not infer missing names or a canonical identity from a memo.
- BANK-FIELD-COMPARE-03: Project only candidate notices or prior linked canonical aliases from the accumulated evidence scope. Multiple notices require explicit display selection; selecting one never assigns a transaction or enables posting. No Bank means no fabricated comparison.
- BANK-FIELD-COMPARE-04: Expandable Bill backlog details show each field with both source values, status and reason; reuse Layout, Card, Button, Typography. No actual editing, sending, ledger mutation or persistent storage. Preserve v13.27 keyboard.
- BANK-FIELD-COMPARE-05: Twelve direct assertions for exact/near/conflicting/missing evidence and no-post; browser regression for multiple Bank selection, missing clock and unchanged new-credit total. Both CI workflows required before merge.

## 148. BANK-OCR-CORRECTION — v13.123
- BANK-OCR-CORRECTION-01: Reuse BillOCRFieldAPI reread/confirm in BankOCRCorrectionAPI; only explicit synthetic image-region evidence, never fill from Bank. Field, bill, fixture region, raw text, operator and reason required.
- BANK-OCR-CORRECTION-02: Verified revision changes exactly one field; retain old/new values and literal reread text. Reject invalid or unchanged candidate, wrong region, stale journal/bill revision and already linked receipt. Existing legacy fixture OCR event path remains unchanged.
- BANK-OCR-CORRECTION-03: Replay validates verified event against previous record and evidence, rejects unrelated field changes; preserve historical cutoff and receipt ownership. New evidence invalidates completeness; explicit scope revision needed before new proposal. No real authorization or posting.
- BANK-OCR-CORRECTION-04: Bill backlog sample previews independent synthetic clock text and confirms explicitly, then recomputes queue; history shows before/after and reason. No actual image reread in this sample; no message or database changes, preserve keyboard v13.27.
- BANK-OCR-CORRECTION-05: 13 direct assertions plus browser regression for preview/confirmation and history, no credit or send. Existing application and PostgreSQL CI required.

## 149. BILL-SINGLE-IMAGE — v13.124
- BILL-SINGLE-IMAGE-01: User scope overrides multi-bill collage workflows: one image = at most one bill. Three accepted source types: saved sharp bill, photographed bill/screen with background, transaction journal. Journal may show multiple rows but one explicitly selected transaction only; never sum or generate multiple receipts from one image.
- BILL-SINGLE-IMAGE-02: Saved image defaults to whole raster. Photo or journal uses one manually chosen bounded rectangle; no automatic multi-region splitting in user-facing intake/engine. Keep original image and existing field reread methods; never use background text, phone status clock, fee or balance as transaction evidence. Crop is manual, not automatic document detection.
- BILL-SINGLE-IMAGE-03: Retain legacy low-level partition and batch APIs for historical regression only; remove collage sample and split controls from current UI. Single scope validates bounds and rejects unsupported type; OCR remains review-only, canPost=false.
- BILL-SINGLE-IMAGE-04: Read-only CHAT source audit 25/11/2026: 577 receipt scan records with linked media and OCR JSON; 357 linked ledger, 187 null transfer timestamp. Stored OCR classifications (not manually validated): successful_transfer_receipt/app_screenshot 110, paper_photo 57, rotated_photo 10, missing layout 235; multiple_transactions 4 overall. Private media bytes not accessible through current connector; no pixel-level corpus validation, no customer image or identifier exported to repository.
- BILL-SINGLE-IMAGE-05: Six direct single-region assertions, seven classic scripts compiled; static/browser regression updated for single image, bounds and three source options. No real posting, messages, production writes or keyboard changes. Full CI before merge.

## 150. BILL-RECOGNITION — v13.125
- BILL-RECOGNITION-01: Image preprocessing/OCR, text recognition, field extraction, Bank reconciliation and ledger posting have separate responsibilities. A bill is an image-form claim of transfer, not proof of funds or image authenticity. Recognition never permits collection.
- BILL-RECOGNITION-02: Configured 13-digit receiving account; exact full match or matching visible suffix of 4/5/6 digits (including masked account). Remove presentation whitespace only, preserve leading zeros. Suffix is a candidate signal only; not an account verification or permission to post. Do not hardcode real account in lab; synthetic 1234567999999.
- BILL-RECOGNITION-03: Require transaction context and account evidence to flag candidate. Exclude amount, memo, reference, sender account and ignored labels from unlabelled number matching. Wrong full account or conflicting explicit recipient evidence blocks candidate; unknown or unlabelled account requires review. Never infer hidden digits or correct ambiguous glyphs.
- BILL-RECOGNITION-04: BillRecognitionAPI.classify returns text-only candidate state, evidence length/type, role and review flag. Existing field parser, Bank matching (including existing suffix policy), receipt keys and ledger posting rules unchanged. canPost=false, canVerifyPayment=false. No new production recognition or real image validation.
- BILL-RECOGNITION-05: Fifteen direct cases, seven classic scripts, static and browser regression for full/partial/wrong account and numeric false positives. Both CI workflows before merge. No customer images/identifiers copied, no production or keyboard changes.

## 151. BILL-FIXED-RECIPIENT — v13.126
- BILL-FIXED-RECIPIENT-01: Receiver account and holder name are fixed configuration, not inferred from OCR. Inject them from trusted configuration. Lab uses synthetic account/name only. Never publish real identifiers.
- BILL-FIXED-RECIPIENT-02: Compare only explicit recipient-name evidence, not sender, memo, arbitrary words or transaction code. Normalize NFC/NFD combining marks, Vietnamese đ, case and presentation whitespace; no fuzzy matching or automatic character substitution. Preserve raw OCR.
- BILL-FIXED-RECIPIENT-03: Exact normalized name supports recognition; missing name does not reject an account-based candidate. Conflicting explicit name returns recipient-conflict; invalid name requires review. Name alone never substitutes for account evidence. Account conflict takes priority. A name match never verifies funds, never bypasses missing clock review, and never posts.
- BILL-FIXED-RECIPIENT-04: Retain existing full 13-digit and visible 4/5/6 suffix rules. No changes to ledger, bank reconciliation, production data, keyboard or old bills. References: https://lodash.com/docs/#deburr (diacritics), https://github.com/naptha/tesseract.js/blob/master/docs/api.md (raw OCR evidence). Vietnamese normalization uses native Unicode, not a new dependency.
- BILL-FIXED-RECIPIENT-05: Static and browser regression for fixed name, accented/case variants, missing/invalid/conflicting names, sender/memo exclusion, name-only and account conflict. Synthetic only; CI and real-image acceptance separate.

## 152. BILL-OCR-SOURCE — v13.127
- BILL-OCR-SOURCE-01: Saved bill defaults to original image; photo and journal require explicit bounded proper subregion before OCR. One image/one region; no automated guessing or collage.
- BILL-OCR-SOURCE-02: Show original crop preview. Optional contrast runs on a new cropped canvas with same dimensions; preserve source and raw OCR, never silently replace original or enlarge geometry. Contrast capped at 12 MP. Source/method change invalidates pending OCR.
- BILL-OCR-SOURCE-03: Retain raw lines and aligned word tokens/confidence separately from semantic fields. If block/text order differs, expose original raw lines and no inferred word mapping. Confidence is review only, never verification. No filtering text based on desired account/name.
- BILL-OCR-SOURCE-04: Reference Tesseract quality guidance https://tesseract-ocr.github.io/tessdoc/ImproveQuality.html (crop background/borders) and https://github.com/naptha/tesseract.js/blob/master/docs/api.md (text and blocks). No new dependencies or production change.
- BILL-OCR-SOURCE-05: Static source scope and raw-line assertions; browser fixture for same-size contrast and retained source. Synthetic data only; actual OCR quality remains unmeasured.

## 153. BILL-SCOPED-FIELD — v13.128
- BILL-SCOPED-FIELD-01: Parent BillOCRFieldAPI owns field routing. readScoped uses existing per-type parsing (account, recipient/sender, amount, date, clock, memo, reference, trace and bank) without changing the full-bill parser or Bank matcher.
- BILL-SCOPED-FIELD-02: Bare value fallback is opt-in only in explicit field-scoped reread. Exactly one unclassified line; no recognized other labels, excluded lines, or competing evidence. Unknown colon label blocked outside date/time. No inference across fields.
- BILL-SCOPED-FIELD-03: Preserve raw text, source, confidence, line and diagnostics. Scoped bare results are proposals; no automatic verification/posting. Invalid glyph or conflicting readings require review. Existing review revision guard and verified-field lock remain.
- BILL-SCOPED-FIELD-04: Image-field reread and explicitly selected manual text reread call same owner. Full image OCR never treats every bare value as a field. Missing clock still requires verification; no change to production, old bills, ledger, keyboard or bank rules.
- BILL-SCOPED-FIELD-05: Regression covers bare clock, amount, account, name, date, memo, reference, wrong-label rejection, multiple lines, invalid glyph, opt-in guard and isolated revision. Synthetic fixtures; CI and real-image accuracy separate.

## 154. BILL-RECIPIENT-PAIR — v13.129
- BILL-RECIPIENT-PAIR-01: Fixed name and account may appear on adjacent unlabelled physical OCR lines in either order. Require exact normalized fixed name, matching full or suffix account evidence, and independent amount/date/time/transfer-heading context.
- BILL-RECIPIENT-PAIR-02: Both lines must be unclassified by the parent parser. Sender, memo, reference and recognized fields cannot be reused. Exclude sender/memo/reference boundary immediately before pair. No substring or fuzzy name inference; no cross-blank-line pairing.
- BILL-RECIPIENT-PAIR-03: Explicit recipient name/account and contradictions take precedence. Adjacent pair is review evidence only; no mutation of parsed fields or bank verification, no posting or retroactive processing.
- BILL-RECIPIENT-PAIR-04: Expose original line numbers and pair evidence class; preserve original OCR. Synthetic configuration only. No changes to bank, ledger or production.
- BILL-RECIPIENT-PAIR-05: Regression for both orders, suffix, accents, wrong name, sender/memo, nonadjacent lines, explicit conflict, no context, and no posting. CI and real-image accuracy separate.

## 155. BILL-UNLABELLED — v13.130
- BILL-UNLABELLED-01: Only unclassified original OCR lines are evaluated for suggestions. Currency marker, strict calendar/clock shapes, fixed account and explicit reference prefix are candidate evidence; no arbitrary numeric or free-text memo inference.
- BILL-UNLABELLED-02: Exclude labelled/ignored fields and adjacent sender/balance/fee/memo/reference boundaries; do not guess phone clock or missing transaction clock. Preserve original line, raw value and candidate reason.
- BILL-UNLABELLED-03: Multiple same-field proposals or existing labelled field are marked ambiguous. Suggestions never change record, verify funds, post or replace existing field review. Date words already handled by parent parser.
- BILL-UNLABELLED-04: No new dependencies, no production or bank matching changes. Synthetic fixed account only; no real identifiers or customer data.
- BILL-UNLABELLED-05: Static and browser regression for money/date/time/account/reference suggestions, ambiguity, exclusion and unchanged parsed records; actual OCR accuracy not certified.

## 156. BILL-ACCOUNT-SPACING — v13.131
- BILL-ACCOUNT-SPACING-01: Remove presentation whitespace within a single numeric account line using ParseAPI.accountDigits; count every visible digit, including final isolated digit. 99999 9 is six digits, not five. Preserve leading zeros and raw text.
- BILL-ACCOUNT-SPACING-02: A wrong final digit is a conflict for explicit recipient evidence; never fall back to matching a shorter prefix or silently drop digits. Masked suffix recognition uses all visible suffix digits.
- BILL-ACCOUNT-SPACING-03: Adjacent numeric physical lines that may represent a wrapped account are review-only; suppress premature partial recognition on the first fragment, retain both raw lines and count; never silently join or post. Explicit recipient scope or matching combined candidate required.
- BILL-ACCOUNT-SPACING-04: No changes to amount, date, bank reconciliation, ledger, old bills or production. Synthetic configured account only.
- BILL-ACCOUNT-SPACING-05: Regression for spaces at every boundary, wrong final digit, masked six-digit suffix, physical wrap and no posting. CI and actual OCR separate.

## 157. BILL-TYPED-OCR — v13.132
- BILL-TYPED-OCR-01: First pass unrestricted. A known value-only crop may use field-specific OCR alphabet; account digits plus mask/space, receiving holder name Latin A–Z/a–z and spaces (unaccented as user specified), money numeric and separators, clock digits/colon/h. Date must support numeric and Vietnamese words; memo and identifiers unrestricted. Do not impose alphabet on whole labelled line.
- BILL-TYPED-OCR-02: Tesseract typed pass uses fresh worker, single-line PSM and whitelist only on separate value-line geometry or explicitly selected value-only manual crop. Existing unrestricted three attempts retained; no state leakage. Same-line label needs verified value-only crop; no guessed geometry.
- BILL-TYPED-OCR-03: Store raw typed output independently; mismatch or inability to read is review only. No automatic letter-to-digit replacement, no fixed account/name used as OCR answer, no posting. Original raw evidence remains authoritative for review.
- BILL-TYPED-OCR-04: Preserve date words and mixed memo; no production, bank, ledger, old bill or keyboard change. Tesseract.js worker.setParameters documentation: https://github.com/naptha/tesseract.js/blob/master/docs/api.md.
- BILL-TYPED-OCR-05: Synthetic static profile and browser worker parameter/cleanup regressions; real OCR corpus not certified.

## 158. BILL-VALUE-GEOMETRY — v13.133
- BILL-VALUE-GEOMETRY-01: A same-line label/value can use typed OCR only if original aligned OCR word tokens reconstruct the entire normalized line exactly and the raw field value is an exact unique trailing token span.
- BILL-VALUE-GEOMETRY-02: Require every word bounding box within the original line, ordered without horizontal overlap; reject missing, reversed, merged label/value or mismatched tokens. Preserve whole-line OCR fallback and existing separate-line value geometry.
- BILL-VALUE-GEOMETRY-03: Crop only measured value token union, never guess by character width. Invalid account digits may still be reread using the exact raw value span. Typed result remains review-only, no posting or bank changes.
- BILL-VALUE-GEOMETRY-04: Upstream Tesseract.js v6 blocks/word bbox and Tesseract segmentation/whitelist docs: https://github.com/naptha/tesseract.js/issues/993 and https://tesseract-ocr.github.io/tessdoc/ImproveQuality.html. No new dependency or private data.
- BILL-VALUE-GEOMETRY-05: Synthetic regression for exact same-line value bounds, invalid glyph, mismatched token, merged label and reversed bbox; browser fixture retained. CI and real corpus acceptance separate.

## 159. BILL-FLOW-AUDIT — v13.134
- BILL-FLOW-AUDIT-01: Run integrated synthetic contract from original OCR text through field parser, recipient recognition, raw Agribank notice parser and existing Bank matcher. Parsing alone never authenticates notice; explicit synthetic verification only in test.
- BILL-FLOW-AUDIT-02: Preserve exact fractional VND, full account including separated final digit, calendar words and transaction clock. Delayed notice and ±1 minute reviewed per existing policy.
- BILL-FLOW-AUDIT-03: Unverified Bank, missing clock, competing bills and posted Bank ID must not be eligible. Recognition conflicts must be resolved before a future production adapter; no implicit integration or posting in this audit.
- BILL-FLOW-AUDIT-04: E2 synthetic cross-layer checks do not certify real OCR pixels, Bank authenticity, durable ledger or production integration. E3–E5 remain pending; do not mark entire project done.
- BILL-FLOW-AUDIT-05: Track remaining acceptance in references/COMPONENT-API.md. No new business API, no production, no keyboard changes.

## 160. BILL-PIXEL-STRESS — v13.135
- BILL-PIXEL-STRESS-01: Evaluate actual synthetic pixels per field; distinguish native Tesseract from browser Tesseract.js, known crop from automatic geometry, and sample pass from customer corpus accuracy.
- BILL-PIXEL-STRESS-02: Independent typed reread disagreement invalidates an agreed-review proposal; unavailable/invalid typed result downgrades agreement to partial review. Retain original proposal and typed raw evidence for diagnosis. Never replace baseline with typed value.
- BILL-PIXEL-STRESS-03: Existing partial/conflict/unreadable baseline is never upgraded by typed result. When no typed reread was attempted, preserve baseline status. All remain verification-only, no post.
- BILL-PIXEL-STRESS-04: Native Tesseract 5.5.0, vie+eng, 5 generated conditions and 35 known field crops measured; 31 exact after presentation whitespace normalization. Actual customer corpus, deployed browser OCR and device acceptance pending.
- BILL-PIXEL-STRESS-05: Regression reproduces observed amount disagreement, blank/invalid typed read and no automatic override; no production, historical bills, keyboard or bank matching changes.

## 161. BILL-BLUR-RESCUE — v13.136
- BILL-BLUR-RESCUE-01: Native benchmark uses 3 synthetic degradations × 7 known fields × 4 methods (84 reads). Contrast + 2x + Pillow unsharp recovers 4/7 severe blur fields vs 3/7 original; not evidence that browser kernel has same accuracy.
- BILL-BLUR-RESCUE-02: Browser rescue is a separate bounded contrast/2x/cross-neighbor sharpen variant on exact value crop only, typed fields only, one extra worker attempt when combined evidence is not agreed-review. Original and three baseline variants remain unchanged.
- BILL-BLUR-RESCUE-03: Rescue text never upgrades state, replaces proposal, supplies missing clock, or authorizes payment. Preserve original and rescue evidence; failed read requires manual review.
- BILL-BLUR-RESCUE-04: Limit source crop 1024×1024, output 2048×2048, preserve alpha and boundaries; no source mutation. Tesseract quality guidance https://tesseract-ocr.github.io/tessdoc/ImproveQuality.html; no new dependency.
- BILL-BLUR-RESCUE-05: Direct/static regression of pixel kernel, bounded input, rescue gating and unchanged review state; CI pending; no customer images or production changes.

## 162. BILL-QUALITY-TRIAGE — v13.137
- BILL-QUALITY-TRIAGE-01: Measure bounded original crop grayscale histogram (1–99 percentile range), mean and neighbor gradient. Record flags only: nearly uniform, low contrast, dark, small crop height, weak edges. Thresholds are provisional heuristics, not accuracy or blur certification.
- BILL-QUALITY-TRIAGE-02: Label scope value vs line; recommend original plus contrast, scale or rescue according to flags. Preserve three baseline reads and existing rescue gate; no automatic choice or skipping evidence based on pixel metrics.
- BILL-QUALITY-TRIAGE-03: Conflict requires comparing original; absent value region requires crop selection. Unreadable critical account, amount, date or clock with known value region produces an editable-purpose draft to request clearer evidence, never sends. No inference of missing values; name/memo/reference review individually.
- BILL-QUALITY-TRIAGE-04: Tesseract quality guidance https://tesseract-ocr.github.io/tessdoc/ImproveQuality.html reviewed; no claimed correspondence between thresholds and production accuracy. No production, customer data, ledger or keyboard changes.
- BILL-QUALITY-TRIAGE-05: Synthetic pixel and advice tests for uniform, contrast, dark, small crop, non-mutation, conflict, missing scope, unreadable clock and no sending/posting. Actual corpus calibration pending.
